- **Installed:** `C:\Users\user\AppData\Local\VoiceStudio (Current User)\omnivoice-studio.exe` (v0.5.2, current-user, no admin).

- **Emotion/expressiveness (OmniVoice default engine):**
  - The #1 lever = **emotionally-performed reference clip** (a flat ref clones flat).
  - Punctuation (`…`, `—`, `!`) + `[pause Nms]` for dramatic beats.
  - `class_temperature` 0.3-0.7 (default 0 = greedy/robotic) → farm 3-5 takes, pin the best seed.
  - `[laughter]` / `[sigh]` tags (⊕ Insert button).
  - `whisper` style (only valid style for the default engine).
  - Turn OFF `postprocess_output` to keep breaths/silences.
  - **DO NOT use** `[happy]`/`[sad]`/`[excited]` tags — the default engine does NOT accept them (will speak the word).
  - **DO NOT use** IndexTTS 2.5 emotion sliders — no Malayalam support.
- **Convert tab (speech-to-speech):** NOT a true voice conversion. It transcribes (Whisper) → re-synthesizes (TTS). Emotion is LOST. For true voice conversion (keep performance, change timbre) → use **RVC**.

### 3. RVC VOICE CONVERSION — CLEAN-UP RECIPE (reduce source leakage)
- **Problem:** RVC output still sounds partly like the user (source leakage).
- **The #1 fix: `--protect 0.0`** (default 0.33 = "keep 33% of original voice" — that's the leak).
- **Full recipe:**
  1. Clean the source (denoise, normalize, mono).
  2. Inference: `--protect 0.0`, `--index-rate 0.7-1.0`, `--f0-method rmvpe`, pitch 0.
  3. **Double pass:** run the output through the same model a 2nd time (protect 0.0 again) — strips residual source character.
  4. Light post: high-pass 80-100 Hz, loudness normalize.
- **A/B test (tomorrow):** same line, 3 ways: (1) single pass protect 0.0, (2) double pass protect 0.0, (3) double pass + post. Listen → pick winner → LOCK the recipe in DECISIONS.md.
- **RVC path:** `D:\models\ai-movie\repos\rvc-webui`, venv `D:\models\ai-movie\envs\rvc\Scripts\python.exe`, model `midhun_v2_40k.pth`.
- **Inference command template:**
  ```
  cd D:\models\ai-movie\repos\rvc-webui
  D:\models\ai-movie\envs\rvc\Scripts\python.exe -m infer.cli --model midhun_v2_40k.pth --input <source.wav> --output <out.wav> --f0-method rmvpe --protect 0.0 --index-rate 0.8
  ```

- ✅ **2026-09-06 VDN-H3 BENCHMARK → OOM AT 14 s / 1MP (NOT viable at production scale) (user: "lets setup the vdn... our objective is to compare with our SLA workflow generation time" → "got OOM, log the details in the memory, i will continue tomorrow"):** **VDN-H3 (Video Delta Net) is NOT viable at our production scale (14 s / 1344×768) on the 32 GB RTX 5090 — it OOMs.** Setup done: `Saganaki22/ComfyUI-VDN-H3` v1.4.0 cloned into `custom_nodes`; int8 ConvRot checkpoint (`drbaph/vdn-minimax-h3-int8-convrot-comfyui`, ~3.49 GB) downloaded to `E:\ComfyUI_windows_portable\ComfyUI\models\vdn\vdn-minimax-h3-int8-convrot-comfyui\`; benchmark workflow `vdn_h3_t2v_benchmark.json` built (validates clean). **Runs:** (1) 124 frames (5 s) = 418.22 s — **INVALID** (submitted while Qwen was active, violates D045); (2) 345 frames (14 s / 1344×768, matching the proven `jaisal_single_shot` production workload) = **OOM at step 0** (184.21 s to fail). **Root cause:** the linear-branch readout (`branch.py:159 frame_statistics`: `torch.matmul(vb.transpose(-1,-2), kf).float()`) holds per-frame K/V statistics for ALL F=102 frames in fp32 → ~31.4 GiB, blowing the 31.84 GiB limit. The vendor's ~95 s reference is **61 frames (F≈19)** — 5× fewer frames — which is why it fits. **VDN's linear-branch memory scales with frame count (worse for longer clips — the opposite of where we need it).** **CONCLUSION: SLA remains the default speed stack (D042). Do NOT adopt VDN for 14 s / 1MP production clips.** **NEW HARD RULE (D045): NEVER run a ComfyUI workflow while a Qwen/llama.cpp session is active — it contaminates benchmarks AND slows both.** **NEXT (tomorrow, if a clean VDN number is still wanted):** set `branch_weights="stream"` (widget idx 5) + `retain_buffers="off"` (idx 6) to cut linear-branch memory, and/or drop to 124 frames / 1280×736; stop the Qwen session first. Full details in `EXPERIMENTS.md` (2026-09-06 VDN-H3 entry) + `DECISIONS.md` D045.

- ✅ **2026-09-03 CONTROLFOLEY AUDIO → v3 SOURCE-AUDIO NOT THE ISSUE; CONTROLFOLEY UNSUITABLE FOR LINE-ART VIDEO (user: "still the same, there is no sound for the actions we mentioned either, and the noise sound is there, first it should make this video soundless or should we make it? maybe thats the issue?"):** the v2 no-water prompt STILL came out as a flat noise bed. **Diagnosis (measured):** (1) **The source video's audio is NOT used by ControlFoley** — `LoadControlFoleyVideo` outputs `{"path", "duration"}` (no audio track); the `ControlFoleyGenerate` `video` input is that dict, and its `video_input`/`images` inputs are NOT connected. The model is NOT generating the footsteps/whistle/splash; it's just outputting a steady noise floor. **Root cause: ControlFoley is trained on realistic video, but the video is a 2D line-art stick-figure animation** — the CLIP video encoder gets almost no realistic visual cues, so it falls back to a generic noise bed. **This is a fundamental limitation: ControlFoley is not suitable for 2D line-art video.** **Options:** (a) **Keep the MiniMax-generated audio** already in the video (it was generated with the natural-sounds prompt — footsteps, whistle, wind, water, birds, whoosh, splash — so its baked-in audio IS the natural-sounds track; simplest path); (b) **Generate action sounds separately** (text-to-audio / SFX library) and sync them manually in a video editor; (c) **ControlFoley is not suitable for this line-art video.** **The input video is now SOUNDLESS** (audio stripped for the ControlFoley workflow); the original with MiniMax audio is still in `output\video\Jaisal_Sketch_Title_00011_.mp4`. **Recommendation: keep the MiniMax-generated audio.** See **D044 update 2026-09-03 (v3)**. **NEXT: user decides — keep the MiniMax audio (recommended) or generate action sounds separately.**

The converter reads widget values from the **internal subgraph nodes** (`definitions.subgraphs[0].nodes`), NOT the instance's `widgets_values`. The LoRA lives on internal node **121** (`LoraLoaderModelOnly`). The template default LoRA (`fl2v_turbo_8step`) is NOT installed → validation error `105/121 lora_name not in options`. **Fix: set `lora_name` on internal node 121 (both `widgets_values[0]` AND `widgets_values_named['lora_name']`) to the installed 4-step 768p LoRA.** Also the instance node's `widgets_values_named` dict is preferred over `widgets_values` by the converter — update BOTH.

## MiniMax H3 Optimization (2026-08-27)

Goal: fastest possible 15s MiniMax H3 video on RTX 5090 (target <15-20 min for 2K; measure at 0.4MP first).

### Key findings
- **"ComfyUI-Kitchen" = `comfy_kitchen`** — a Python INT8 attention backend, ALREADY installed in the embedded Python. NOT a node pack.
- **Star7 pack** (`minimax-h3-chunk-star7`, cloned to custom_nodes) wires Comfy Kitchen INT8 into ComfyUI. Node: `MiniMaxH3ActivationChunkStar7` (attention_backend=`comfy_kitchen_int8`, chunk_tokens=8192, mlp=8192, qkv=4096). Also `MiniMaxH3ReferenceVideoLoadStar7`, `MiniMaxH3LoadImageScaleStar7`.
- **`triton` is MISSING** → KJNodes `PathchSageAttentionKJ` CRASHES (this is why the original mini-max-jaisal-test.json failed at node 45). sageattention/flash_attn/xformers all missing too.
- **`torch 2.12.0+cu130`** (CUDA 13.0). ComfyUI runs with `--use-pytorch-cross-attention` (native = "existing" backend).
- **Turbo LoRA** `minimax_h3_fl2v_turbo_4step_v1.0_768p_comfyui_bf16.safetensors` (1.96 GB) — downloaded to main path loras/. 4-step = fastest.
- **fl2va model** (`minimax_h3_fl2va_pruned_int8_convrot`) = first-last-frame → perfect for the drone-ascent (start frame → final frame).
- `MiniMaxH3ReferenceToVideo` is a CORE node (comfy_extras/nodes_minimax_h3.py), model-agnostic.
- Core also has `MiniMaxH3MemoryEfficientSageAttentionPatch` (triton-free sage option).
- **ResolutionSelector** 0.4MP 16:9 = 864×480. 15s @24fps = 362 frames (17k+5 grid).
- **RTXVideoSuperResolution** (comfyui_nvidia_rtx_nodes) = 2× ULTRA upscaler for 2K.

### New optimized workflow: jaisal_drone_optimized.json
Chain: UNETLoader(fl2va) → LoraLoaderModelOnly(fl2v_turbo_4step) → **MiniMaxH3ActivationChunkStar7 (CK INT8)** → BasicScheduler(simple,8,1) + BasicGuider → SamplerCustomAdvanced(res_multistep) → VAEDecode → **RTXVideoSuperResolution(2× ULTRA)** → CreateVideo(24fps) → SaveVideo(video/Jaisal_Drone).
- MiniMaxH3ReferenceToVideo: ref_image_0=jaisal_mm_start.png, ref_image_1=jaisal_mm_final.png, 864×480, 362 frames, drone-ascent prompt.
- VALIDATED (valid=true, no partner nodes, no credit spend). NOT yet run.
- **BUG FIXED**: stale `widgets_values_named` (new frontend prefers named over positional) — all 7 modified nodes now consistent.

### Models needed (all in main path E:\ComfyUI_windows_portable\ComfyUI\models)
- diffusion_models/minimax_h3_fl2va_pruned_int8_convrot.safetensors ✅
- text_encoders/qwen3vl_32b_minimax_h3_nvfp4_awq.safetensors ✅
- vae/minimax_h3_video_vae_fp16.safetensors ✅
- vae/minimax_h3_audio_vae_fp32.safetensors ✅
- loras/minimax_h3_fl2v_turbo_4step_v1.0_768p_comfyui_bf16.safetensors ✅ (downloaded)

### ComfyUI restart note
ComfyUI is NOT under comfy-cli control (portable launcher) → MCP restart_comfyui fails. Restart manually: Stop-Process the main.py PID, relaunch `.\python_embeded\python.exe -s ComfyUI\main.py --windows-standalone-build --reserve-vram 4 --disable-pinned-memory --use-pytorch-cross-attention --enable-manager`. Restarting kills in-flight jobs.
